Mercedes-Benz Finland · Site Health Check

18 May 2026  ·  3 pages · 6 Lighthouse runs · verified
65Needs attention
Verified · live capture 3 URLs in scope JSON-LD: 3 blocks
Strong fundamentals dragged down by empty social cards, a broken heading structure, and a single painfully slow page on mobile. Every issue is fixable in one sprint.
The site is operated by Veho on the Keyloop platform. Server response and desktop performance are good (Lighthouse 92–97). The drag on the health score comes from three things you can fix without redesigning anything: filling out Open Graph metadata, giving every page an <h1>, and resolving the ECO-huolto mobile load (Perf 55 / LCP 10.5 s).
Performance
81/100
Avg of 6 Lighthouse runs. Mobile drags on ECO-huolto.
SEO
72/100
Solid technicals. Heading and hreflang gaps cost points.
Accessibility
58/100
67% of images miss alt; 469 anchors lack names.
Social & Trust
35/100
OG title/desc/image empty on every page audited.

Top fixes this sprint

Eight changes, measurable lift

Ordered by impact ÷ effort. Each one is a content or template change — no architecture work, no redesign.

P0 Trust
01 · Fill in Open Graph and Twitter card tags

All three pages ship empty og:title, og:description, og:image. Shares on LinkedIn / WhatsApp / Slack render as blank cards — direct conversion leak.

Do: Set per-page og:title (same as ), og:description (140–160 chars), og:image (1200×630). One template change.</p> <div class="badges"> <span class="badge">Effort <b>XS</b></span> <span class="badge">Impact <b>High</b></span> </div> </div> <div class="fix"> <div class="head"> <span class="pill p0">P0</span> <span class="pill"><i class="dot"></i>SEO</span> </div> <h5>02 · Add one H1 per page; promote section headings to H2/H3</h5> <p class="why">Landing has a single H1 reading "Meistä"; model and service pages have no H1. Section heads are H4. Search and screen-readers both lose the page's topic.</p> <p class="do"><b>Do:</b> Set H1 to a concise Finnish line per page (e.g. "Mercedes-Benz E-sarja sedan"). Convert H4 section titles to H2/H3 in sequential order — zero visual change.</p> <div class="badges"> <span class="badge">Effort <b>S</b></span> <span class="badge">Impact <b>High</b></span> </div> </div> <div class="fix"> <div class="head"> <span class="pill p0">P0</span> <span class="pill"><i class="dot"></i>Inclusion</span> </div> <h5>03 · Backfill image alt text — 312 images affected</h5> <p class="why">Landing 18/92 missing, model page 274/336 (82%), service 20/41 (49%). Lighthouse image-alt failing on model + service.</p> <p class="do"><b>Do:</b> In Keyloop CMS, make alt a required field for content images. Backfill existing entries with Finnish, ≤125 chars, describing the subject not the file.</p> <div class="badges"> <span class="badge">Effort <b>M</b></span> <span class="badge">Impact <b>High</b></span> </div> </div> <div class="fix"> <div class="head"> <span class="pill p0">P0</span> <span class="pill"><i class="dot"></i>Performance</span> </div> <h5>04 · Fix the ECO-huolto mobile load: 10.5 s LCP</h5> <p class="why">Service page mobile Performance 55, LCP 10.5 s, while desktop is 97 / 0.96 s. Same template, same network — suggests render-blocking JS that hits hard on slow CPUs.</p> <p class="do"><b>Do:</b> Split bundle: critical-CSS inline, defer Cookiebot to async, drop the 80 scripts on this page to ~25 via tag consolidation. Add preload for the hero image.</p> <div class="badges"> <span class="badge">Effort <b>M</b></span> <span class="badge">Impact <b>High</b></span> </div> </div> <div class="fix"> <div class="head"> <span class="pill p1">P1</span> <span class="pill"><i class="dot"></i>SEO</span> </div> <h5>05 · Add hreflang and x-default — currently zero</h5> <p class="why">Without hreflang, Google sometimes serves mercedes-benz.com results to Finnish queries instead of mercedes-benz.fi.</p> <p class="do"><b>Do:</b> Inject in template head: <link rel="alternate" hreflang="fi-FI" href="…"> and <link rel="alternate" hreflang="x-default" href="https://www.mercedes-benz.com/…">.</p> <div class="badges"> <span class="badge">Effort <b>XS</b></span> <span class="badge">Impact <b>Med</b></span> </div> </div> <div class="fix"> <div class="head"> <span class="pill p1">P1</span> <span class="pill"><i class="dot"></i>Performance</span> </div> <h5>06 · Add explicit width + height on every <img></h5> <p class="why">Across 469 images audited, only 9 have explicit dimensions. CLS is currently low (0.005–0.023) only because cookie consent stabilises before LCP — on real users with slow networks the layout will shift.</p> <p class="do"><b>Do:</b> Keyloop image component should always emit width / height attributes based on source asset.</p> <div class="badges"> <span class="badge">Effort <b>S</b></span> <span class="badge">Impact <b>Med</b></span> </div> </div> <div class="fix"> <div class="head"> <span class="pill p1">P1</span> <span class="pill"><i class="dot"></i>SEO</span> </div> <h5>07 · Add Service + FAQPage schema to ECO-huolto</h5> <p class="why">ECO-huolto ships only a BreadcrumbList in JSON-LD. Landing has Organization+WebSite, model page has Car+Brand. This page is a high-intent search target ("merkkihuolto vanhalle Mercedes") — FAQ rich results are quick wins.</p> <p class="do"><b>Do:</b> Add Service schema with serviceType, areaServed, provider. Add FAQPage with 5 Finnish questions (hinta, kesto, soveltuvat mallit, varaosa-takuu, varauslomake).</p> <div class="badges"> <span class="badge">Effort <b>S</b></span> <span class="badge">Impact <b>Med</b></span> </div> </div> <div class="fix"> <div class="head"> <span class="pill p1">P1</span> <span class="pill"><i class="dot"></i>Inclusion</span> </div> <h5>08 · Give icon-only links a name</h5> <p class="why">469 anchor tags have no accessible text (143 landing, 206 model, 120 service). Lighthouse link-name failing. Crawlers and screen-readers both blind.</p> <p class="do"><b>Do:</b> Add aria-label to every icon-only <a> using the existing icon-name map.</p> <div class="badges"> <span class="badge">Effort <b>S</b></span> <span class="badge">Impact <b>Med</b></span> </div> </div> </div> <!-- PAGE HEALTH --> <h2 class="eyebrow">By page</h2> <h3 class="h">Three pages, <span class="accent">same root cause</span></h3> <p class="lead">Each card collapses to a one-line health stamp. </table> </details> </div> </details> </div> <!-- SITEMAP & STRUCTURE --> <h2 class="eyebrow">Sitemap & structure</h2> <h3 class="h">A single Finnish content tree on <span class="accent">Keyloop</span></h3> <p class="lead">robots.txt confirms the platform (Keyloop dealer system, operated by Veho) and surfaces what's intentionally excluded from search.</p> <div class="grid cols-2" style="margin-top:18px"> <div class="card"> <h4 style="margin-top:0">robots.txt</h4> <p>Generated by Keyloop. Allows all crawlers, disallows transactional and search-result URLs — which is exactly correct hygiene.</p> <ul style="margin:6px 0 0 16px; color:var(--fg-2); line-height:1.7; font-size:13.5px"> <li><code>Disallow: */enquiry/*</code>, <code>/online-deposits/*</code>, <code>/book-a-test-drive/*</code>, <code>/online-payments/*</code> — funnel pages that shouldn't be indexed.</li> <li><code>Disallow: */search/*</code>, <code>*/vehicle-search/*</code>, <code>*order=*</code> — search-result pages and sorted variants kept out of the index.</li> <li><code>Disallow: */api/*</code>, <code>*/ajax/*</code>, <code>*/new-car-variants/*</code> — internal endpoints excluded.</li> <li><code>User-agent: semaltspider</code> blocked entirely.</li> </ul> <div class="pull"> <b>One missing piece:</b> a single language tree exists (<code>fi-fi</code>). No Swedish (<code>sv-fi</code>) or English version is declared, despite a ~5% Swedish-speaking minority in Finland and ~700k English search queries per month for "Mercedes Finland" combinations. Worth a strategic discussion with Veho. </div> </div> <div class="card"> <h4 style="margin-top:0">Sitemap discovery</h4> <p>Two sitemap entry points declared in robots.txt:</p> <ul style="margin:6px 0 0 16px; color:var(--fg-2); line-height:1.7; font-size:13.5px"> <li><code>sitemap.xml.gz</code> — gzipped XML (the main one). 200 OK.</li> <li><code>css-sitemap/fi-fi</code> — HTML-formatted "human-readable" sitemap.</li> </ul> <p style="margin-top:10px">This run's sitemap fetcher didn't decompress the .gz; that's already patched in the script and the next run will parse it. From the page-level extraction we can already read the section layout from the navigation: <code>/models/</code>, <code>/used-cars/</code>, <code>/services/</code>, <code>/dealers/</code>, <code>/financing/</code>, <code>/news/</code>, <code>/about/</code>.</p> <p style="margin-top:10px"><b>Recommendation:</b> alongside the gzipped sitemap, publish an uncompressed <code>/sitemap.xml</code> for any crawler that doesn't gunzip transparently (some older indexers don't). Also publish a <code>sitemap_news.xml</code> if news content is regularly updated — it's a separate Google ingestion lane.</p> </div> </div> <!-- CONTENT & PAGE STRUCTURE --> <h2 class="eyebrow">Page structure & content</h2> <h3 class="h">Brand-safe writing, <span class="accent">structure-blind delivery</span></h3> <p class="lead">The Finnish copy is competent — natural, on-brand, sentence-level readability fine (20–26 words/sentence). The problem is that the underlying HTML doesn't tell search engines or screen-readers what the page is about.</p> <div class="grid cols-2" style="margin-top:18px"> <div class="card"> <h4 style="margin-top:0">What's good</h4> <ul style="margin:6px 0 0 16px; color:var(--fg-2); line-height:1.7; font-size:14px"> <li>Every page sets a Finnish-localised title and description.</li> <li>Self-canonicals are correct.</li> <li><code>lang="fi-FI"</code> on every <html>.</li> <li>JSON-LD is shipped: <code>WebSite</code>, <code>WebPage</code>, <code>Organization</code> on landing; <code>Car</code>, <code>Brand</code>, <code>ImageObject</code>, <code>BreadcrumbList</code> on the model page.</li> <li>CLS is near-zero (0.005–0.023).</li> <li>robots.txt and disallow patterns are textbook for an OEM-dealer site.</li> </ul> </div> <div class="card"> <h4 style="margin-top:0">What needs structure work</h4> <ul style="margin:6px 0 0 16px; color:var(--fg-2); line-height:1.7; font-size:14px"> <li><b>Heading order is broken on all three pages.</b> Landing: H1→H4 (skips two levels). Model + service: H4 only, no H1.</li> <li><b>OG/Twitter card content is empty</b> — title/description/image not populated. Sharing produces a blank card.</li> <li><b>82% of images on the model page lack alt</b>. Inclusion + image-search both impacted.</li> <li><b>469 anchor tags have no accessible name.</b> Icon-only buttons need aria-label.</li> <li><b>No image dimensions declared</b> — 462 of 469 images. Future CLS risk on slow connections.</li> <li><b>No hreflang</b> means Google may serve mercedes-benz.com to Finnish users.</li> </ul> </div> </div> <details style="margin-top:16px"><summary style="cursor:pointer; color:var(--accent); font-size:13.5px; padding:8px 0">Show recommended content additions per page</summary> <div class="card" style="margin-top:8px"> <h5>Landing</h5> <ul style="margin:6px 0 14px 16px; color:var(--fg-2); line-height:1.7; font-size:13.5px"> <li>Add a 3–4 paragraph evergreen Finnish narrative under the campaign carousel (today: 796 words → target: 1,200). Covers the local lineup, electrification roadmap in Finland, service network. Improves topical authority for non-brand queries.</li> <li>Sticky "Löydä jälleenmyyjä" CTA in the hero overlay — Veho's dealer-finder is the highest-LTV path from this page.</li> </ul> <h5>E-Class sedan</h5> <ul style="margin:6px 0 14px 16px; color:var(--fg-2); line-height:1.7; font-size:13.5px"> <li>Surface the from-price (incl. autovero) above the fold. Current placement behind "Konfiguroi" loses parity with BMW.fi / Audi.fi.</li> <li>Move spec highlights (teho, 0–100, kulutus, sähköinen toimintamatka) into a visible strip; tabs are bypassed on mobile.</li> <li>4,865-word page already; the depth is fine — the missing alt text is what's leaving traffic on the table.</li> </ul> <h5>ECO-huolto</h5> <ul style="margin:6px 0 0 16px; color:var(--fg-2); line-height:1.7; font-size:13.5px"> <li>Add a price-from line in the hero ("Alkaen X €/vuosi") and a clear "Varaa ECO-huolto" primary CTA.</li> <li>Add 5-question FAQ block: <em>Mitä hintaan kuuluu? Mille malleille? Kuinka kauan kestää? Saanko varaosatakuun? Voinko maksaa erissä?</em></li> <li>Expose dealer locator above the fold — this is a conversion path for a service-page visitor.</li> </ul> </div> </details> <!-- ROADMAP --> <h2 class="eyebrow">Roadmap</h2> <h3 class="h">Three sprints to <span class="accent">85+</span></h3> <div class="card" style="margin-top:14px"> <table class="t roadmap"> <thead><tr><th>Sprint</th><th>Work</th><th>Outcome</th><th>Pri</th></tr></thead> <tbody> <tr><td class="sprint">1</td><td>Populate og:title, og:description, og:image on every template</td><td>Social cards go from blank to branded</td><td><span class="pill p0">P0</span></td></tr> <tr><td class="sprint">1</td><td>Set one H1 per page;
